10924 orig=22904 U28407 *** A special note about internal error number 1007 ***
10925 orig=22905 U28408 Experience suggests that a common cause of i.e. 1007
10926 orig=22906 U28409 is unreliable memory or other hardware.  The 1007 assertion
10927 orig=22907 U28410 just happens to cross-check the results of huge numbers of
10928 orig=22908 U28411 memory reads/writes, and so acts (unintendedly) as a stress
10929 orig=22909 U28412 test of your memory system.
10930 orig=22910 U28413 I suggest the following: try compressing the file again,
10931 orig=22911 U28414 possibly monitoring progress in detail with the -vv flag.
10932 orig=22912 U28415 * If the error cannot be reproduced, and/or happens at different
10933 orig=22913 U28416   points in compression, you may have a flaky memory system.
10934 orig=22914 U28417   Try a memory-test program.  I have used Memtest86
10935 orig=22915 U28418   (www.memtest86.com).  At the time of writing it is free (GPLd).
10936 orig=22916 U28419   Memtest86 tests memory much more thorougly than your BIOSs
10937 orig=22917 U28420   power-on test, and may find failures that the BIOS doesn't.
10938 orig=22918 U28421 * If the error can be repeatably reproduced, this is a bug in
10939 orig=22919 U28422   bzip2, and I would very much like to hear about it.  Please
10940 orig=22920 U28423   let me know, and, ideally, save a copy of the file causing the
10941 orig=22921 U28424   problem -- without which I will be unable to investigate it.
